                    local v9 = 1
                    local v10 = 0
                    local v11 = 0

                    while v9 <= v7 do
                        local v12 = sub(v5, v9, v9)
                        local v13 = v3[v12]

                        if v13 then
                            v10 = v10 + v13 * 64 ^ (3 - v11)
                            v11 = v11 + 1

                            if v11 == 4 then
                                v11 = 0
                                insert(
                                    v8,
                                    char(floor(v10 / 65536), floor(v10 % 65536 / 256), v10 % 256)
                                )
                                v10 = 0
                            end
                        elseif v12 == "=" then
                            insert(v8, char(floor(v10 / 65536)))

                            if v9 >= v7 or sub(v5, v9 + 1, v9 + 1) ~= "=" then
                                insert(v8, char(floor(v10 % 65536 / 256)))
                            end

                            break
                        end

                        v9 = v9 + 1
                    end

                    v1[i] = concat(v8)
                elseif v6 == "g" then
                    v5 = sub(v5, 2)
                    local v14 = len(v5)
                    local v15 = {}
                    local v16 = 1

                    while v16 <= v14 do
                        local v17 = v14 - v16 + 1
                        local v18 = v17 >= 5 and 5 or v17
                        local v19 = 0
                        local v20 = v18 > 1

                        for j = 0, 4 do
                            local v21

                            if j < v18 then
                                v21 = v4[sub(v5, v16 + j, v16 + j)]

                                if not v21 then
                                    v20 = false
                                    break
                                end
                            else
                                v21 = 84
                            end

                            v19 = v19 * 85 + v21
                        end

                        if v20 then
                            local v22 = floor(v19 / 16777216) % 256
                            local v23 = floor(v19 / 65536) % 256
                            local v24 = floor(v19 / 256) % 256
                            local v25 = v19 % 256

                            if v18 == 5 then
                                insert(v15, char(v22, v23, v24, v25))
                            elseif v18 == 4 then
                                insert(v15, char(v22, v23, v24))
                            elseif v18 == 3 then
                                insert(v15, char(v22, v23))
                            elseif v18 == 2 then
                                insert(v15, char(v22))
                            end
                        end

                        v16 = v16 + v18
                    end

                    v1[i] = concat(v15)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if not game:IsLoaded() then
        game.Loaded:Wait()
    end

    if getgenv and getgenv().MalaphasUnload then
        pcall(getgenv().MalaphasUnload)
    end

    localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
    currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    v26 = true
end

local vector = Vector3.new(109, -680, 1184)

local function f2(p3)
    if not p3 then
        return false
    elseif p3 == localPlayer then
        return true
    elseif v29.TeamCheck == false then
        return false
    else
        local getPlayerFromCharacter = nil

        if typeof(p3) == "Instance" then
            if p3:IsA("Player") then
                getPlayerFromCharacter = p3
            else
                if localPlayer.Character and (p3 == localPlayer.Character or p3:IsDescendantOf(
                    localPlayer.Character
                )) then
                    return true
                end

                getPlayerFromCharacter = players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(
                    p3:IsA("Model") and p3 or p3:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
                )
            end
        end

        if getPlayerFromCharacter and localPlayer.Team and getPlayerFromCharacter.Team and localPlayer.Team == getPlayerFromCharacter.Team then
            return true
        end

        if getPlayerFromCharacter then
            local teamID = localPlayer:GetAttribute("TeamID") or localPlayer:GetAttribute("Team")
            local teamID2 = getPlayerFromCharacter:GetAttribute("TeamID")

            local team = teamID2
            team = teamID2 or getPlayerFromCharacter:GetAttribute("Team")

            local v31 = teamID ~= nil
            local v32 = team

            if v31 and v32 ~= nil and teamID == v32 then
                return true
            end
        end

        local character = getPlayerFromCharacter and getPlayerFromCharacter.Character or typeof(p3) == "Instance" and (p3:IsA(
            "Model"
        ) and p3 or p3:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model"))

        local character2 = localPlayer.Character

        if character and character2 then
            local teamID3 = character2:GetAttribute("TeamID") or character2:GetAttribute("Team")
            local teamID4 = character:GetAttribute("TeamID") or character:GetAttribute("Team")

            if teamID3 ~= nil and teamID4 ~= nil and teamID3 == teamID4 then
                return true
            end

            if character:FindFirstChild("TeammateLabel", true) or character:FindFirstChild(
                "AllyLabel", true
            ) then
                return true
            end

            return false
        end

        return false
    end
end

local function f3()
    local character3 = localPlayer.Character
    local humanoidRootPart = character3 and character3:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if not humanoidRootPart then
        return false
    end

    return (humanoidRootPart.Position - vector).Magnitude < 450
end

        return true
    end
end

local function f10()
    local character4 = localPlayer.Character

    if not character4 then
        return false
    else
        for index4, value5 in ipairs(character4:GetChildren()) do
            if value5:IsA("Tool") then
                return true
            end
        end

        local viewModels = workspace:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")

        local firstPerson = viewModels
        firstPerson = viewModels and viewModels:FindFirstChild("FirstPerson")

        if firstPerson and #firstPerson:GetChildren() > 0 then
            for index5, value6 in ipairs(firstPerson:GetChildren()) do
                if value6:IsA("Model") or value6:IsA("BasePart") then
                    return true
                end
            end

            return false
        end

        return false
    end
end

local function f11(p7)
    pcall(
        function()
            local playerScripts2 = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
            local playerModule2 = playerScripts2 and playerScripts2:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")

            if playerModule2 then
                local getControls2 = require(playerModule2):GetControls()

                if getControls2 then
                    if p7 then
                        getControls2:Enable()
                    else
                        getControls2:Disable()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    )
end

local function f12(p8)
    mainWindow.Visible = p8

    if p8 then
        cframe = currentCamera.CFrame
        fieldOfView = currentCamera.FieldOfView

        userInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
        userInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true

        f11(false)
        local character5 = localPlayer.Character

        local humanoidRootPart2 = character5
        humanoidRootPart2 = character5 and character5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if humanoidRootPart2 then
            humanoidRootPart2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                0, math.min(humanoidRootPart2.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0), 0
            )
        end

        pcall(
            function()
                contextActionService:BindActionAtPriority(
                    "MalaphasMenuFreeze",
                    function()
                        return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
                    end, false, Enum.ContextActionPriority.High.Value + 5000,
                    Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement, Enum.UserInputType.Touch, Enum.KeyCode.W,
                    Enum.KeyCode.A, Enum.KeyCode.S, Enum.KeyCode.D, Enum.KeyCode.Space,
                    Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift, Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl, Enum.KeyCode.Up,
                    Enum.KeyCode.Down, Enum.KeyCode.Left, Enum.KeyCode.Right
                )
            end
        )
    else
        cframe = nil
        fieldOfView = nil
        f11(true)
        pcall(
            function()
                contextActionService:UnbindAction("MalaphasMenuFreeze")
            end
        )
    end
end

local function f24()

        pcall(mouse1click)
        return
    end

        pcall(
            function()

                mouse1release()
            end
        )

        return
    end

    local v69, v70

    if virtualInputManager then
        local viewportSize = currentCamera and currentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
        v69 = math.floor(viewportSize.X * 0.5)
        v70 = math.floor(viewportSize.Y * 0.5)

        if userInputService.TouchEnabled and not userInputService.MouseEnabled and virtualInputManager.SendTouchEvent then
            pcall(
                function()
                    virtualInputManager:SendTouchEvent(0, 0, v69, v70)
                    task.wait(0.01)
                    virtualInputManager:SendTouchEvent(0, 2, v69, v70)
                end
            )

            return
        end

        if virtualInputManager.SendMouseButtonEvent then
            pcall(
                function()
                    virtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(v69, v70, 0, true, workspace, 0)
                    task.wait(0.01)
                    virtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(v69, v70, 0, false, workspace, 0)
                end
            )

            return
        end

        return
    end
end

local function f31(p38, p39, p40, p41)

        p41
    )

    local parent7 = v83 and v81 - v83.time < 0.06 and v83.target and v83.target.Parent

        return v83.target
    else

            local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
            raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

        end

        local v85 = nil

        local character6 = localPlayer.Character
        local humanoidRootPart3 = character6 and character6:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local position4 = currentCamera.CFrame.Position
        local v86 = p38 == nil
        local lookVector = currentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
        local v87 = v86 or p38 >= 999
        v44.staticRayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {character6, currentCamera}

        for index14, value16 in ipairs(f17()) do
            local character7 = value16.Character

            local humanoid = character7
            humanoid = character7 and character7:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

            local humanoidRootPart4 = character7
            humanoidRootPart4 = character7 and character7:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if character7 and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoidRootPart4 then
                if v87 or (humanoidRootPart4.Position - position4):Dot(lookVector) > -5 then
                    local deflecting = character7:GetAttribute("Deflecting") or character7:FindFirstChild(
                        "Deflect"
                    ) or character7:FindFirstChild("KatanaDeflect")

                    local shielded = character7:GetAttribute("Shielded") or character7:FindFirstChild(
                        "Shield"
                    ) or character7:FindFirstChild("EnergyShield")

                    local spawnImmunity = character7:GetAttribute("SpawnImmunity")

                    local forceField = spawnImmunity
                    forceField = spawnImmunity or character7:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField")

                    local v88 = false

                    if f2(value16) or f2(character7) then
                        v88 = true
                    end

                    if v29.SilentIgnoreDeflecting and deflecting then
                        v88 = true
                    end

                    if v29.SilentIgnoreShielded and shielded then
                        v88 = true
                    end

                    if v29.SilentVulnerableOnly and forceField then
                        v88 = true
                    end

                    if not v88 then
                        local head = character7:FindFirstChild("Head")

                        local hitboxHead = head
                        hitboxHead = head or character7:FindFirstChild("HitboxHead")

                        local upperTorso = character7:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or humanoidRootPart4

                        if p40 == "Head" then
                            v84 = hitboxHead or upperTorso
                        elseif p40 == "Body" then
                            v84 = upperTorso or hitboxHead
                        elseif p40 == "Closest" then
                            if hitboxHead and upperTorso then
                                local v89, v90 = currentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(
                                    hitboxHead.Position
                                )
                                local v91, v92 = currentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(
                                    upperTorso.Position
                                )

                                if v90 and v92 then
                                    v84 = (Vector2.new(v89.X, v89.Y) - getMouseLocation).Magnitude <= (Vector2.new(
                                        v91.X, v91.Y
                                    ) - getMouseLocation).Magnitude and hitboxHead or upperTorso
                                elseif v90 then
                                    v84 = hitboxHead
                                elseif v92 then
                                    v84 = upperTorso
                                else
                                    v84 = hitboxHead
                                end
                            else
                                v84 = hitboxHead or upperTorso
                            end
                        else
                            v84 = hitboxHead or upperTorso
                        end

                        if v84 then
                            local v93 = false
                            local huge2 = math.huge

                            if v87 then
                                local magnitude = humanoidRootPart3 and (v84.Position - humanoidRootPart3.Position).Magnitude or (v84.Position - position4).Magnitude

                                if magnitude <= (p38 or math.huge) then
                                    v93 = true

                                    if p41 == "Health" then
                                        huge2 = humanoid.Health
                                    else
                                        huge2 = magnitude
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                local v94, v95 = currentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(v84.Position)

                                if v95 and v94.Z > 0 then
                                    local magnitude2 = (Vector2.new(v94.X, v94.Y) - getMouseLocation).Magnitude

                                    if magnitude2 <= (p38 or math.huge) then
                                        v93 = true

                                        if p41 == "Health" then
                                            huge2 = humanoid.Health
                                        elseif p41 == "Distance" and humanoidRootPart3 then
                                            huge2 = (v84.Position - humanoidRootPart3.Position).Magnitude
                                        else
                                            huge2 = magnitude2
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end

                            if v93 and huge2 < huge then
                                if p39 then
                                    local raycast2 = workspace:Raycast(
                                        position4, v84.Position - position4, v44.staticRayParams
                                    )

                                    if not raycast2 or raycast2.Instance:IsDescendantOf(character7) then
                                        huge = huge2
                                        v85 = v84
                                    end
                                else
                                    v85 = v84
                                    huge = huge2
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        v44.targetCache[v82] = {target = v85, time = v81}
        return v85
    end
end

do
    local function f32()
        local v123 = {}

        for key3, value18 in pairs(v29) do
            if typeof(value18) == "EnumItem" then
                v123[key3] = {
                    __type = "EnumItem", enumType = tostring(value18.EnumType), name = value18.Name,
                }
            elseif typeof(value18) == "Color3" then
                v123[key3] = {__type = "Color3", r = value18.R, g = value18.G, b = value18.B,}
            elseif typeof(value18) == "Vector3" then
                v123[key3] = {__type = "Vector3", x = value18.X, y = value18.Y, z = value18.Z,}
            else
                v123[key3] = value18
            end
        end

        return v123
    end

    local function f33(p48)
        local v124 = {}

        for key4, value19 in pairs(p48) do
            if type(value19) == "table" and value19.__type == "EnumItem" then
                local v125 = Enum[value19.enumType]

                if v125 and v125[value19.name] then
                    v124[key4] = v125[value19.name]
                end
            elseif type(value19) == "table" and value19.__type == "Color3" then
                v124[key4] = Color3.new(value19.r, value19.g, value19.b)
            elseif type(value19) == "table" and value19.__type == "Vector3" then
                v124[key4] = Vector3.new(value19.x, value19.y, value19.z)
            else
                v124[key4] = value19
            end
        end

        return v124
    end

    function v126.readStore()
        local v127, v128 = pcall(
            function()
                if readfile and isfile and isfile("malaphas_config.json") then
                    return httpService:JSONDecode((readfile("malaphas_config.json")))
                end

                return nil
            end
        )

        if v127 and type(v128) == "table" then
            if type(v128.profiles) == "table" then
                v126.profiles = v128.profiles
                v126.autoload = tostring(v128.autoload or "default")
            else
                v126.profiles = {default = v128}
                v126.autoload = "default"
            end
        else
            v126.profiles = {}
            v126.autoload = "default"
        end

        for key5, value20 in pairs(v126.defaultProfiles) do
            if not v126.profiles[key5] then
                local v129 = {}

                for key6, value21 in pairs(value20) do
                    v129[key6] = value21
                end

                v126.profiles[key5] = v129
            end
        end

        local v130 = {}

        for key7, value22 in pairs(v126.profiles) do
            table.insert(v130, tostring(key7))
        end

        table.sort(v130)

        if #v130 == 0 then
            v130 = {"default", "rage"}
        end

        v126.names = v130
        local v131 = false

        for index16, value23 in ipairs(v126.names) do
            if value23 == v126.autoload then
                v131 = true
                break
            end
        end

        if not v131 then
            v126.autoload = "default"
        end

        v126.current = v126.autoload

        if not (isfile and isfile("malaphas_config.json")) then
            v126.writeStore()
        end
    end

    function v126.writeStore()
        if not writefile then
            return false
        else
            local v132 = {autoload = v126.autoload or "default", profiles = v126.profiles}

            return (pcall(
                function()
                    writefile("malaphas_config.json", (httpService:JSONEncode(v132)))
                end
            ))
        end
    end

    function v126.applyProfile(p49, p50)
        local current = p49
        local v133 = tostring

        if not p49 then
            current = v126.current or "default"
        end

        local v134 = v133(current)
        local v135 = v126.profiles[v134]

        if not v135 then
            if p50 then
                f8("MALAPHAS", "Profile not found: " .. v134, "WARN", 2.5)
            end

            return false
        end

        for key8, value24 in pairs((f33(v135))) do
            local v136 = key8
            local v137 = value24
            v29[v136] = v137

            if v78[v136] and v78[v136].Set then
                pcall(
                    function()
                        v78[v136].Set(v137)
                    end
                )
            end
        end

        f7()
        v126.current = v134

        if v126.nameInput and v126.nameInput.Set then
            v126.nameInput.Set(v134)
        end

        if v126.dropdown and v126.dropdown.Set then
            v126.dropdown.Set(v134)
        end

        if p50 then
            f8("MALAPHAS", "Loaded profile: " .. v134, "SUCCESS", 2.5)
        end

        return true
    end

    function v126.saveProfile(p51)
        local get = p51 or v126.nameInput and v126.nameInput.Get and v126.nameInput.Get()

        if not get or get:gsub("%s+", "") == "" then
            get = v126.current or "default"
        end

        local sS = get:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")

        v126.profiles[sS] = f32()
        v126.current = sS

        local v138 = {}

        for key9, value25 in pairs(v126.profiles) do
            table.insert(v138, tostring(key9))
        end

        table.sort(v138)

        v126.names = v138
        v126.writeStore()

        if v126.dropdown and v126.dropdown.SetOptions then
            v126.dropdown.SetOptions(v126.names, sS)
        end

        if v126.nameInput and v126.nameInput.Set then
            v126.nameInput.Set(sS)
        end

        f8("MALAPHAS", "Saved profile: " .. sS, "SUCCESS", 2.5)
    end

    function v126.setAutoload(p52)
        local current2 = p52 or v126.current or "default"

        if not v126.profiles[current2] then
            v126.profiles[current2] = f32()
        end

        v126.autoload = current2
        v126.writeStore()

        if v126.autoLoadLabel then
            v126.autoLoadLabel.Text = "Auto-load on start: " .. current2
        end

        f8("MALAPHAS", "Auto-load set to: " .. current2, "SUCCESS", 2.5)
    end

    function v126.deleteProfile(p53)
        local current3 = p53 or v126.current

        if #v126.names <= 1 then
            f8("MALAPHAS", "Cannot delete only remaining profile.", "WARN", 2.5)
            return
        else
            v126.profiles[current3] = nil
            local v139 = {}

            for key10, value26 in pairs(v126.profiles) do
                table.insert(v139, tostring(key10))
            end

            table.sort(v139)
            v126.names = v139

            if v126.autoload == current3 then
                v126.autoload = v126.names[1]
            end

            v126.current = v126.names[1]
            v126.writeStore()

            if v126.dropdown and v126.dropdown.SetOptions then
                v126.dropdown.SetOptions(v126.names, v126.current)
            end

            if v126.nameInput and v126.nameInput.Set then
                v126.nameInput.Set(v126.current)
            end

            if v126.autoLoadLabel then
                v126.autoLoadLabel.Text = "Auto-load on start: " .. v126.autoload
            end

            v126.applyProfile(v126.current, false)
            f8("MALAPHAS", "Deleted profile: " .. current3, "INFO", 2.5)
            return
        end
    end

    v126.readStore()

    local rightCol10 = config:FindFirstChild("RightCol")

    local function f34()
        local v142 = {}

        for key11, value27 in pairs(v51) do
            for index17, value28 in ipairs({"LeftCol", "RightCol"}) do
                local findFirstChild = value27:FindFirstChild(value28)

                if findFirstChild then
                    for index18, value29 in ipairs(findFirstChild:GetChildren()) do
                        if value29:IsA("Frame") then

    local v194 = {}

    local function f42(p77)
        if not p77 then
            return "Unarmed"
        else
            local v195 = tick()
            local v196 = v194[p77]

            if v196 and v195 - v196.time < 0.5 then
                return v196.name
            else
                local name = "Fighter"

                for index28, value40 in ipairs(p77:GetChildren()) do
                    if value40:IsA("Tool") then
                        name = value40.Name
                        break
                    end
                end

                if name == "Fighter" then
                    local rightHand = p77:FindFirstChild("RightHand")

                    local rightArm = rightHand
                    rightArm = rightHand or p77:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")

                    if rightArm then
                        for index29, value41 in ipairs(rightArm:GetChildren()) do
                            if (value41:IsA("Weld") or value41:IsA("Motor6D")) and value41.Part1 and value41.Part1.Parent and value41.Part1.Parent ~= p77 and value41.Part1.Parent ~= workspace then
                                name = value41.Part1.Parent.Name
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                v194[p77] = {name = name, time = v195}
                return name
            end
        end
    end

    local function f43(p78)
        if p78.isShown then

            p78.box.Visible = false
            p78.nameLbl.Visible = false
            p78.distLbl.Visible = false
            p78.headDot.Visible = false

                    value42.Visible = false
                end
            end

            if p78.tracerLine then
                p78.tracerLine.Visible = false
            end
        end
    end
